# Scandal Season — Season Four, Chapters 1–3: Full Scene Detail

## Chapter 1 — "The Winning Hand"

**STATUS: FULL DRAFT** — awaiting Beth review.

**Sources:** `ten-season-storyboard-beats.md` (Season 4 section — highest authority), `ten-season-plot-arc.md` (Season 4 section), `prose-standards.md` (locked standard), `season-one-chapters-1-3-pilot.md` (pilot file format), `season-one-chapter-04.md` (chapter anatomy).

**Format key.** [D] dialogue-heavy · [F] fashion-selection (2–3 diegetic options, no coin cost, remembered) · [C] dressing-for-climax ritual (direction + occasion brief, decomposed micro-decisions, story morsels, reveal coda) · [T] texture micro-beat · plot beat (untagged) · Gazette sting · cliffhanger. ★ marks the chapter's exactly 3 key decisions. Animation: Shared throughout — L1 is not a tentpole; the ritual uses the shared ritual presentation.

**Turns.** Every ordinary scene carries 7–10 numbered player turns `(T# · type)` inline — look closer, stance, tone, choice, social maneuver, dialogue, or remembered micro-decision — each closed with a remembered note. Outcomes converge: the game remembers the means (tone, relationship movement, Gazette flavor), never as a plot branch. ★ key-decision scenes carry the decision's 3 options as their turns; the Gazette sting is a single reading turn. The [C] ritual's direction + twenty 5-coin pins are its 21 turns; the ritual runs long and sits outside the 350–450-word scene budget.

**Canon applied:** 1815. Rose Hartwell rebuilds Hartwell Park and earns her place; unhurried about marriage, honorable in method. Suitors honorable; villains sly, never overt. Lucien de Valcourt is the Network's London face — offstage in Ch1–10: never appears, never speaks; his name may be spoken, his presence felt only through agents, empty chairs, withdrawn invitations, opera glasses. In S4 he is the acquirer, nothing more. Mrs. Nance Bell is the Gazette's publicly named proprietor from the first edition — the paper judges and narrates, never a mystery, never a prize. The library is the house's paper memory. Rose carries no flowers for the confrontation arc; laurel is reserved for the Assembly (Ch25). Iron rule holds: time earns everything money can. Crowns (premium) / coins (soft). Energy is the only throttle. No Crown SKU touches the ritual. The solicitor is Mr. Quill; Mr. Silas Vane is an agent — never confuse them. Lady Agatha Drummond is absent this season; her held second installment (£5,000) hangs over it.

**Occasion:** The Season's opening — the first subscription assembly at Almack's; the Directresses' gate. **Preparation ritual:** the season's opening gown — command, not apology. **Purse shelf:** the Season's opening costs, honestly faced.

**What changes for Rose:** She enters the Season holding the libel war's winnings; the library becomes her war room; the first hall-tray note arrives and is set aside sealed; the Directresses' cool nod becomes the Season's first verdict.

### L1.S1 · [T] · Rose Hartwell, Aunt Letitia Hartwell
*Purpose: the morning after the libel war — the winning hand, counted; the Season opens. (Turns: 8)*
> *(Morning, and the Gazette folded on the breakfast tray like a receipt. The Lantern is broken. Three weeks of gutter ink, answered in the open and answered clean, and Mrs. Nance Bell's verdict sits at the column's foot in her own dry hand: the town has read it all, and the town has made up its mind. Rose Hartwell, the woman the libel couldn't kill. She pours chocolate and counts her winnings the way Augusta taught her — once for the sum, once for the nerve.)*
> "Read it again, darling. I want to watch your face do the arithmetic." — Letitia
> (T1 · look closer) *the Gazette — folded, the verdict's column; the tray — chocolate, toast, the morning's calm inventory; the arithmetic — won in the open, owing nothing to the judge.* — remembered: *The winnings, counted.*
> "It was won in the open. That is the whole of it." — Rose
> (T2 · tone) *quiet — the victory, held steady; dry — the Hartwell refusal to preen; bright — the Season, opening like a door.* — remembered: *She refuses the preen.*
> "The town made up its mind, my love, which is rarer than being right." — Letitia
> (T3 · remembered micro-decision) *keep the Gazette — the receipt, filed; fold it small — the victory, pocketed; leave it open — let the house read it.* — remembered: *The receipt, filed.*
> (T4 · stance) *as the victor — steady, the hand won; as the soldier — the war, not the battle, is the point; as the Hartwell — winnings are for spending.* — remembered: *She spends forward.*
> "And now the Season. Shall we be magnificent, or shall we be careful?" — Letitia
> (T5 · dialogue) *"Magnificent — carefully."; "Careful — magnificently."; "Both, Aunt. That is what the Season is for."* — remembered: *The Season, chosen.*
> (T6 · social maneuver) *set the household's tone — victory, worn lightly; set the aunt's — let her preen a little; set her own — the work ahead, priced.* — remembered: *The tone, set.*
> (T7 · look closer) *the chocolate — cooling, the morning's one patience; the window — London, the Season's first light; the tray — the paper's fold, exact, like a hand of cards laid down.* — remembered: *The morning, read.*
> (T8 · choice) *read the verdict once more — the savoring; read the fashion column — the campaign's next front; close the paper — the day's work, begun.* — remembered: *The day, begun.*
> *(She closes the paper. The toast is buttered, the chocolate drunk, and the winning hand lies face-up on the breakfast tray — and Rose understands, with the particular clarity that follows a won fight, that winning was the easy part. The town has made up its mind about the libel. The Season will make up its mind about her. Those are different minds, and the second one keeps longer hours.)*
> *The Gazette's fold is exact as a laid-down hand of cards, and the morning smells of chocolate and ink, and somewhere below stairs the household is already stirring with the particular energy of a house that has decided to go to town.*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L1.S2 · [T] · Rose, Letitia
*Purpose: the household comes to town — trunks, orders, the campaign's logistics. (Turns: 8)*
> *(The house in town wakes the way an army wakes: trunks in the hall, the housekeeper's inventory unrolled like a battle plan, Letitia giving orders about shawls with the authority of a field marshal. The Season does not begin at Almack's. It begins here, in the hall, with the trunks.)*
> "The blue trunk to my room, the hat boxes to Miss Rose, and if anyone touches the Sèvres I shall have them transported." — Letitia
> (T1 · look closer) *the trunks — the campaign's baggage, literal; the inventory — unrolled, the housekeeper's battle plan; the Sèvres — threatened with transportation, entirely safe.* — remembered: *The household, mustered.*
> (T2 · stance) *muster with the aunt — two generals, one hall; inspect the troops — the servants, the silver; hold the stairs — the high ground, kept.* — remembered: *She takes command.*
> (T3 · tone) *brisk — the logistics, loved; amused — the Sèvres, the transportation; fond — the aunt in full cry.* — remembered: *The cry, enjoyed.*
> "A house in town is a statement, darling. We are making one." — Letitia
> (T4 · dialogue) *"Then let it be a true one."; "Statements, Aunt — I make them daily."; "What does this one say?"* — remembered: *The statement, drafted.*
> (T5 · social maneuver) *as the mistress — the orders, given; as the niece — the aunt, permitted her marshalship; as the strategist — the hall, mapped for the weeks ahead.* — remembered: *The weeks, mapped.*
> (T6 · remembered micro-decision) *the hat boxes — upstairs, the campaign's crown; the blue trunk — Letitia's, the aunt's colors; the Sèvres — untouched, untransported, safe.* — remembered: *The baggage, placed.*
> (T7 · look closer) *the hall — trunks, paper, purpose; the stairs — the ascent, staged; the light — the Season's, through the fanlight.* — remembered: *The house, awake.*
> (T8 · choice) *unpack the papers first — the war room, founded; unpack the gowns first — the campaign's colors; unpack nothing — the general surveys before deploying.* — remembered: *The papers, first.*
> *(She chooses the papers. The trunks can wait; the gowns can wait; the Sèvres, threatened with transportation, will survive the morning. What cannot wait is the work, and the work is paper, and the paper is waiting in the library with the dust of three generations on it. She leaves Letitia to her marshalship and climbs the stairs toward it, and the house — awake, mustered, making its statement — hums around her like a hive that has decided on its queen.)*
> *The hat boxes ascend in state, the blue trunk follows, and the fanlight lays the Season's first light across the hall floor in a long clean bar — and Rose takes the stairs two at a time, because the library is waiting, and the winning hand is no good to anyone still sitting on the breakfast tray.*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L1.S3 · [T] · Rose (alone)
*Purpose: the library — first sight; dust, the paper memory, the war room's raw material. (Turns: 8)*
> *(The library has been waiting. Three generations of Hartwell paper, shelved in the order in which life happened to it — which is to say, no order at all. Dust lies on the high shelves like a second, greyer set of books. The desk is vast. The light from the tall windows falls on the blotter in a clean parallelogram, and Rose stands in the doorway and understands, all at once, that this room is the campaign's true headquarters, and that it has been waiting for someone to say so.)*
> (T1 · look closer) *the shelves — three generations, unsorted; the dust — the grey second library, undisturbed; the desk — vast, the blotter's clean parallelogram of light.* — remembered: *The room, surveyed.*
> (T2 · remembered micro-decision) *open a window — the dust, disturbed at last; leave the dust — the archive's dignity; sneeze — the archive's welcome.* — remembered: *The window, opened.*
> (T3 · stance) *as the heir — the paper, inherited; as the general — the room, claimed; as the scholar — the shelves, read at a glance.* — remembered: *The room, claimed.*
> (T4 · tone) *reverent — the generations, shelved; practical — the sorting, priced; delighted — the campaign's true headquarters, found.* — remembered: *The delight, filed.*
> (T5 · look closer) *the blotter — the light's parallelogram, exact; the inkstand — dry, the pen's long retirement; the chair — the general's, awaiting its general.* — remembered: *The desk, readied.*
> (T6 · social maneuver) *with the dust — "You have had your century; it is mine now." (unsaid); with the shelves — the generations, greeted; with the room — the headquarters, declared.* — remembered: *The declaration, made.*
> (T7 · remembered micro-decision) *touch nothing yet — the survey, complete first; touch everything — the hunger, admitted; touch the desk — the claim, physical.* — remembered: *The desk, touched.*
> (T8 · choice) *begin with Rackham — the instrument's leavings; begin with the old ledgers — the family's own memory; begin with the windows — light first, paper second.* — remembered: *Rackham, first.*
> *(She touches the desk. The wood is cool under her palm, the blotter's parallelogram of light exact as a map's border, and the dust — disturbed at last by the opened window — turns slowly in the sunbeam like something considering whether to settle again. It will not settle. She has decided. The library is no longer a room where books are kept. It is a room where paper is interrogated, and the paper had better have its answers ready.)*
> *The chair waits. The inkstand is dry. The shelves hold three generations of unsorted life, and Rose stands among them the way a general stands on a surveyed field — and smiles, because the field is hers, and the enemy has been kind enough to leave his papers behind.*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L1.S4 · plot beat · Rose, Augusta (Dowager Marchioness of Ravencroft)
*Purpose: Augusta's counsel — what the winning hand bought, and what paper costs. (Turns: 8)*
> *(Augusta's morning room. The Dowager has read the Gazette, folded it with more precision than the paper deserves, and now regards her granddaughter over the spectacles with the expression of a woman pricing a victory she did not pay for.)*
> "You won in the open, child. The town noticed. Now the town will watch what winning does to you, which is the more interesting spectacle." — Augusta
> (T1 · look closer) *the spectacles — lowered, the pricing in progress; the Gazette — folded with precision, the victory filed; the morning room — the Dowager's own headquarters, for comparison.* — remembered: *The pricing, undergone.*
> (T2 · tone) *steady — the victory, held; dry — "More interesting than the libel, Grandmama?"; grave — the watching, understood.* — remembered: *She holds steady.*
> "The Network is done hiding behind cutouts, Rose. The gutter failed them. They will not use the gutter twice." — Augusta
> (T3 · remembered micro-decision) *note the name — the Network, spoken plainly; note the warning — the gutter, retired as a weapon; file both — intelligence, priced.* — remembered: *The warning, filed.*
> (T4 · stance) *as the soldier — the next weapon, anticipated; as the granddaughter — the counsel, received; as the Hartwell — the open, preferred.* — remembered: *The counsel, taken.*
> "And the library?" — Rose
> (T5 · dialogue) *"And the library — what does it cost me?"; "The paper remembers, Grandmama — what does it charge?"; "Tell me what the library is for."* — remembered: *The question, asked.*
> "Paper remembers everything, child, and charges nothing until you need it to forget. Then it is ruinous." — Augusta
> (T6 · look closer) *the phrase — paper remembers; the price — forgetting, ruinous; the Dowager — who has paid it, once.* — remembered: *The price, noted.*
> (T7 · social maneuver) *as the pupil — the lesson, taken; as the heir — the library, accepted as inheritance; as the campaigner — the cost, budgeted.* — remembered: *The budget, set.*
> (T8 · remembered micro-decision) *curtsy — the counsel's full stop; the spectacles — raised, the dismissal; the stairs — the library, waiting.* — remembered: *She leaves counseled.*
> *(She curtsies. The spectacles rise, the dismissal is complete, and the morning room releases her back to her own headquarters — which is smaller, dustier, and entirely hers. Paper remembers everything. She climbs toward the library thinking of the price of forgetting, and of the Dowager's face when she named it — the face of a woman who has paid it, once, and never speaks of the receipt.)*
> *The staircase is short and the counsel is long, and Rose carries it the way she carries everything Augusta gives her: carefully, like a loaded pistol, with the safety understood to be entirely theoretical.*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L1.S5 · [D] · Rose, Mr. Quill
*Purpose: the solicitor — Rackham's papers, delivered; the instrument's leavings, boxed. (Turns: 8)*
> *(Mr. Quill arrives with the punctuality of a man whose whole profession is keeping other people's time, and with him three boxes of Sir Giles Rackham's papers — the instrument's leavings, tied with tape that has gone yellow at the edges. Dead men leave paper the way living men leave debts: comprehensively.)*
> "Sir Giles's papers, Miss Hartwell. His clerk boxed them as he found them, which is to say, as Sir Giles left them." — Mr. Quill
> (T1 · look closer) *the boxes — three, tape yellowed; the clerk's order — Sir Giles's order, which is none; the solicitor — punctual, professional, carrying other people's time.* — remembered: *The delivery, received.*
> (T2 · tone) *grave — the dead, acknowledged; brisk — the work, begun; dry — "Comprehensively, I take it."* — remembered: *The gravity, kept.*
> "Comprehensively is the word, miss. Sir Giles was thorough in all things, including ruin." — Mr. Quill
> (T3 · remembered micro-decision) *accept the boxes — the leavings, claimed; count them — three, the inventory begun; thank the solicitor — the profession, honored.* — remembered: *The boxes, claimed.*
> (T4 · stance) *as the heir — the instrument's leavings, hers by conquest; as the investigator — the boxes, unopened, promising; as the lady — the solicitor, thanked properly.* — remembered: *The conquest, filed.*
> (T5 · dialogue) *"Was he thorough in honesty, Mr. Quill?"; "Three boxes — is that the whole of him?"; "What does a dead man's paper weigh, do you think?"* — remembered: *The questions, asked.*
> "Honesty was not among the things he was thorough in, miss. As to the weight — paper is heavier than stone, in my experience. It sinks slower and crushes flatter." — Mr. Quill
> (T6 · look closer) *the phrase — heavier than stone; the solicitor's face — professional, with something underneath; the boxes — the weight, imagined.* — remembered: *The phrase, kept.*
> (T7 · social maneuver) *walk him to the door — courtesy, the profession's due; detain him — one question more; release him — the boxes, the room, the afternoon.* — remembered: *He is walked out.*
> (T8 · remembered micro-decision) *stack the boxes — the library's first acquisition; cut no tape — the opening, a ceremony for later; close the door — the room, the paper, the quiet.* — remembered: *The acquisition, shelved.*
> *(She walks him out. The hall is empty of trunks now, the household's muster complete, and Mr. Quill's retreating back is the last of the morning's deliveries. She returns to the library and stands before the three boxes — tape yellowed, the clerk's order which is no order — and does not cut the tape. Not yet. Some openings deserve a witness, and some deserve a plan, and these deserve both. The boxes wait. Paper is patient. So, she is learning, is she.)*
> *The tape's yellowed edges catch the parallelogram of light, and the library holds its breath around three dead boxes — and Rose, who has learned to count winnings and costs in the same sitting, stands among the dust and does the arithmetic of a dead man's thoroughness.*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L1.S6 · [T] · Rose, Lord Julian Ashcombe
*Purpose: Julian — the Network's fellow victim; he fears for her, not the plan. (Turns: 8)*
> *(The call comes in the afternoon's calling hours, as calls do — but Julian Ashcombe does not sit. He stands by the library's tall windows with his hat in his hands, turning it the way a man turns a thing he is trying not to break, and the first thing he says is not about the libel war at all.)*
> "You won. I am glad of it, and I am frightened by it in equal measure, and I have decided to tell you the second part first." — Julian
> (T1 · look closer) *the hat — turned, unbroken, barely; the stance — by the windows, not seated; the telling — the second part first, the honesty of it.* — remembered: *The honesty, received.*
> (T2 · tone) *gentle — the fear, honored; dry — "Equal measure is very like you."; direct — "Tell me the first part too."* — remembered: *She honors it.*
> "The first part is that I am glad. The second is that I know what these people do to people, Rose. I have watched it done. I do not fear for your plan. I fear for you." — Julian
> (T3 · remembered micro-decision) *hear the distinction — the plan, the person; hold it — the fellow victim, speaking; answer it — the fear, not dismissed.* — remembered: *The distinction, held.*
> (T4 · stance) *as the friend — the fear, permitted; as the campaigner — the plan, defended; as the woman — both, at once.* — remembered: *Both, held.*
> "Then fear with me, not for me. It is better company." — Rose
> (T5 · dialogue) *"Then fear with me, not for me."; "I am harder to break than their last instruments."; "Your fear is noted, my lord — and overruled."* — remembered: *The fear, answered.*
> (T6 · social maneuver) *take his hand — the friend, steadied; keep the desk between them — the campaigner, stationed; offer chocolate — the hostess, deflecting.* — remembered: *The hand, taken.*
> (T7 · look closer) *the hat — still now, the turning stopped; the windows — the light, the afternoon's; the man — the fellow victim, the friend, the fear.* — remembered: *The stillness, noted.*
> (T8 · remembered micro-decision) *keep his fear — filed, not dismissed; keep his gladness — the first part, kept; keep him — the friend, the lane, the bond.* — remembered: *He is kept.*
> *(She takes his hand. The hat is still now, the turning stopped, and the library's dust turns in the parallelogram of light around two people who have both been used by the same machine and come out the other side with their hands still their own. He fears for her, not the plan. She files the distinction the way she files everything — carefully, and where she can find it again when it matters.)*
> *The afternoon light holds them a moment at the tall windows — the fellow victim and the woman who won in the open — and his hand is warm in hers, and the fear sits between them like a third person at the call, acknowledged and, for now, outvoted.*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L1.S7 · [T] · Rose, Comte Laurent de Varenne
*Purpose: Laurent — the French instinct; survive beautifully, fight later; her English directness terrifies him. (Turns: 8)*
> *(Laurent arrives with flowers for Letitia and compliments for the house and a careful, practiced lightness that Rose has learned to read the way she reads the Gazette — for what is printed between the lines. They walk in the square's garden, because he suggested it, because rooms have ears and gardens only have sparrows.)*
> "The town says you are magnificent, chérie. The town is a gossip and a flatterer, but on this one point, it happens to be right." — Laurent
> (T1 · look closer) *the lightness — practiced, the reading between the lines; the garden — sparrows, not ears; the walk — suggested, the caution in it.* — remembered: *The caution, read.*
> (T2 · tone) *wry — "The town flatters so rarely — I shall frame it."; direct — "And what do you say, Laurent?"; light — the banter, returned.* — remembered: *The banter, returned.*
> "I say be careful. These people —" He stops. Starts again. "The French have a saying: the beautiful swimmer does not fight the current. She crosses where it is narrow." — Laurent
> (T3 · remembered micro-decision) *hear the stop — the unsaid, priced; hear the saying — survive beautifully, fight later; hear the fear — exposure, the family kind.* — remembered: *The fear, priced.*
> (T4 · stance) *as the Englishwoman — directness, the native tongue; as the friend — his fear, permitted its accent; as the strategist — the current, measured.* — remembered: *The current, measured.*
> "I am English, Laurent. I fight the current where it is widest, in daylight, with witnesses." — Rose
> (T5 · dialogue) *"I fight where it is widest."; "Your beautiful swimmer would drown in my river."; "Then teach me to cross narrowly — after I have won widely."* — remembered: *The directness, stated.*
> (T6 · social maneuver) *tease him — the fear, lightened; press him — the unsaid, coaxed; walk on — the sparrows, the garden, the peace.* — remembered: *The walk, continued.*
> (T7 · look closer) *his face — the terror, politely masked; the sparrows — indifferent, the garden's; the path — the crossing, narrow or wide.* — remembered: *The terror, seen.*
> "You terrify me, you know. It is the most English thing about you, and I adore it, and it will be the death of my composure." — Laurent
> (T8 · remembered micro-decision) *keep the terror — the honesty, filed; keep the adoration — the lane, warmed; keep the walk — the garden, the sparrows, the peace.* — remembered: *The walk, kept.*
> *(They walk on. The sparrows go about their sparrow business, indifferent to currents wide or narrow, and Laurent's careful lightness settles back over him like a coat he never quite takes off — but Rose has seen underneath it, the way she has learned to see underneath everything this Season: the French instinct, survive beautifully and fight later, braced against her English directness like a man braced against weather. He adores it. It terrifies him. Both are true. That is the whole of the lane, and she walks it with him, sparrow-paced, and does not ask him to choose.)*
> *The garden gate clicks behind them, and his bow is perfect, and the terror sits in his eyes like a well-bred guest — present, polite, and entirely unignorable.*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L1.S8 · [T] · Rose, Major Lord Henry Beaumont
*Purpose: Henry — duty against love; there is no legal shape to this war. (Turns: 8)*
> *(Henry comes on horseback, as he does everything — direct, in daylight, with the mud of the Row still on his boots. He finds her in the library among the unopened boxes, and stands in the doorway the way a soldier stands at a map table: ready to be useful, uncertain of the terrain.)*
> "I have been thinking about your war, Miss Hartwell. A soldier likes to know the shape of a thing — the objective, the lawful authority, the line of retreat." — Henry
> (T1 · look closer) *the boots — the Row's mud, the daylight; the doorway — the soldier at the map table; the boxes — unopened, the terrain.* — remembered: *The terrain, surveyed.*
> (T2 · tone) *wry — "Afraid the library offers none of those."; gentle — the soldier, honored; direct — "There is no lawful authority, Henry."* — remembered: *The honesty, given.*
> "That is what troubles me. There is no magistrate to carry this to, no commission, no colors. I was trained for wars with shapes." — Henry
> (T3 · remembered micro-decision) *hear the trouble — duty, the honest kind; name it — love against duty, the lane's divide; hold it — the soldier, the man, the friend.* — remembered: *The divide, named.*
> (T4 · stance) *as the friend — his trouble, permitted; as the campaigner — the shapeless war, defended; as the woman — the man, not the uniform.* — remembered: *The man, seen.*
> "Then love me into a shapeless war, or love me out of it. Those are the shapes on offer." — Rose
> (T5 · dialogue) *"Love me into it, or out of it."; "The war has no shape — I do. Hold to that."; "Duty never had to be tidy, Henry — only kept."* — remembered: *The shapes, offered.*
> (T6 · social maneuver) *show him the boxes — the terrain, shared; keep him in the doorway — the soldier, stationed; ask his counsel — the map table, used.* — remembered: *The counsel, sought.*
> (T7 · look closer) *his face — the duty, the love, the arithmetic; the mud — drying on the boots, the daylight's; the boxes — the unopened, the waiting.* — remembered: *The arithmetic, witnessed.*
> "I shall hold to you, then. It is the only shape I am certain of." — Henry
> (T8 · remembered micro-decision) *keep the certainty — the lane, deepened; keep the trouble — the duty, honored; keep him — the soldier, the friend.* — remembered: *He is kept.*
> *(He holds to her. The mud dries on his boots, the library's dust turns in the light, and the unopened boxes sit between them like a map table neither of them can quite read yet — but he has chosen his shape, and it is her, and the choosing is the whole of the lane. Duty against love, and love — for now, in daylight, with the Row's mud still on his boots — carrying the day.)*
> *The doorway empties. The boxes wait. And Rose stands among the paper with a soldier's certainty filed beside a Frenchman's terror and a fellow victim's fear — three lanes, three weathers, and the Season barely a week old.*
*Animation: Shared.*
### L1.S9 · [T] · Rose, Mrs. Octavia Wren
*Purpose: Octavia — the only one who asks what the confrontation is for. (Turns: 8)*
> *(Octavia comes to sit, not to call — there is a difference, and the library understands it. She settles into the chair opposite the desk with her sketchbook unopened on her knee, and looks at Rose with the particular attention of a woman who paints people and therefore sees them.)*
> "You won beautifully. Now tell me —" She pauses, the painter's pause, choosing the line. "What is the winning for?" — Octavia
> (T1 · look closer) *the sketchbook — unopened, the attention undivided; the pause — the line, chosen; the question — the only one nobody else has asked.* — remembered: *The question, heard.*
> (T2 · tone) *honest — "I don't know yet."; dry — "Winning, Octavia. The for comes later."; thoughtful — the question, turned over.* — remembered: *The honesty, given.*
> "Everyone else asks how. Julian asks if you'll survive it. Laurent asks if it can be survived beautifully. Henry asks if it's lawful." She smiles. "I am asking what it's for." — Octavia
> (T3 · remembered micro-decision) *hear the catalogue — the lanes, divided; hear the smile — the friendship, the love; hear the question — the for, unanswered.* — remembered: *The catalogue, kept.*
> (T4 · stance) *as the friend — the question, permitted; as the campaigner — the for, postponed; as the woman — the not-knowing, admitted.* — remembered: *The not-knowing, owned.*
> "If the answer is vengeance, say so now, and I shall still sit here. But say it." — Octavia
> (T5 · dialogue) *"It isn't vengeance."; "It might be. I haven't decided."; "It's the Park. It's always been the Park."* — remembered: *The answer, attempted.*
> (T6 · social maneuver) *let her sketch — the painter, permitted; stop her — the sitting, refused; ask her — "What do you see?"* — remembered: *The painter, asked.*
> (T7 · look closer) *her eyes — the seeing, the painter's; the unopened book — the portrait, postponed; the friendship — the courage to name the cost.* — remembered: *The courage, noted.*
> "I see a woman deciding. That is the most interesting face there is." — Octavia
> (T8 · remembered micro-decision) *keep the question — the for, filed; keep the friendship — the witness, loved; keep the deciding — the face, worn.* — remembered: *The witness, kept.*
> *(She does not open the sketchbook. The question sits on the desk between them like a fourth box, unopened and unopenable, and Rose understands that of all the lanes, this is the one that asks the most and offers the least comfort — and that she would not trade it. What is the winning for. The library's dust turns in the light. She has no answer. The not-knowing sits in the room like a guest she has decided, for now, to keep.)*
> *Octavia rises, the sketchbook still unopened, and touches Rose's shoulder as she passes — the painter's benediction, the friend's — and the door closes on the question, which stays.*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L1.S10 · [T] · Rose, Lavinia Crane

### L1.S13 · plot beat · Rose (alone)
*Purpose: ★ KEY DECISION 1/3 — THE LIBRARY (the war room's founding order). How Rose organizes the paper memory. (Turns: 3 — the decision)*
> *(Evening. The library's lamps are lit, the three boxes stand open at last — the tape cut in a small ceremony with Letitia holding the lamp like an acolyte — and Rackham's papers lie across the desk in the clerk's non-order, which is to say, the order of a thorough man's chaos. Beyond them, the shelves: three generations of Hartwell paper, waiting for their general. The war room is founded tonight. The only question is the order of battle.)*
> *(She stands among it all with a pencil behind her ear and Augusta's phrase in her head — paper remembers everything — and understands that how she sorts the memory is how she will fight the war. The debtors. The dates. The London interests. Three orders of battle, and the lamp burning, and Letitia — holding the lamp, the acolyte — waiting to see which general her niece intends to be.)*
> *(The destination is fixed — the Network's money, mapped; who profited from the Hartwells' ruin; what the principal wants next — but the means are hers. She prices them: by debtor — every name that ever owed or was owed, the human map, the grudges and the gratitudes; by date — the ruin's chronology, the speculation's seasons, cause laid beside effect like bodies on a field; by London interest — the money's present tense, where it sits now, who holds it, what it wants next. Each order finds different truths. Each makes a different war.)*
*★ KEY DECISION 1/3 — The library:*
- **By debtor** — *The human map.* Every name that owed or was owed; grudges and gratitudes, filed where she can find them.
- **By date** — *The ruin's chronology.* Cause beside effect, season by season; the speculation's anatomy, laid bare.
- **By London interest** — *The money's present tense.* Where it sits now, who holds it, what it wants next; the war as it stands.
> *(The game remembers the choice — and the means: the human map, the chronology, or the present tense. The library is founded on her order. The paper will remember it her way.)*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L1.S15 · [C] · Rose (alone)
*Purpose: the season's opening ritual — direction + occasion brief, ~20 micro-decisions, story morsels between pins, reveal coda. (Turns: 21)*
> **Occasion brief (published):** "The Season's first subscription assembly at Almack's, this week. The dress code: the Directresses' gate — command, not apology; nerve, not glitter. The gown must pass the gatekeepers standing straighter. **Editorial criterion: command.**"
>
> **A. The Hartwell** — the Park's colors; deep green silk, sober and landed. Motif: the Hartwell oak sprig, restrained at the hem — the estate, declared. Thread: **gold** (the landed, stated) / **copper** (the Park's autumn) / **matching silk** (the sober, exact). Colorway: deep green + cream + warm gold. Accessories: the Hartwell blue ribbon; pearl drop earrings; a simple gold pendant; cream kid gloves.
> **B. The Victor** — the woman the libel couldn't kill; slate lutestring, severe. Motif: plain strength — no motif, the severity is the statement — the victory, worn. Thread: **silver** (the severe, edged) / **steel grey silk** (the severe, matched) / **ivory whitework** (the severe, lit). Colorway: slate + silver + ivory. Accessories: grey kid gloves; a small silver hair comb; no pendant — the throat, plain and unbought.
> **C. The Season's Own** — ivory figured muslin; the column's darling. Motif: figured sprigs, delicate — taste, courted. Thread: **gold** (the taste, gilded) / **pale blue silk** (the taste, cooled) / **ivory whitework** (the taste, lit). Colorway: ivory + pale blue + gold. Accessories: pale blue ribbon; pearl drop earrings; a simple pearl pendant; white kid gloves.
>
> *(System: commit to a direction — remembered for the assembly. Then the pins begin. Each pin costs 5 coins; no Crown SKUs; jewels from owned pieces only, never a ritual step.)*
>
> (T1 · choice — the direction) *She commits: A, B, or C — the assembly's thesis, remembered.*
>
> **Pin 1 (T2 · micro-decision) — Dress silhouette:** the assembly gown's line — empire, unhurried; command, cut into silk. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 2 (T3 · micro-decision) — Bodice cut:** the evening's architecture — fitted, honest, the command's foundation. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 3 (T4 · micro-decision) — Bodice fabric:** silk, lutestring, or fine muslin — the evening's honest weaves. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 4 (T5 · micro-decision) — Bodice color:** the direction's ground — deep green, slate, or ivory — command's canvas. *5 coins.*
>
> *(Story morsel: Élise's girl, pinning: "The Directresses look for nerve, miss, not glitter. Madame says a girl was turned away last Season for too many spangles. *Spangles, miss.* The gate has opinions.")*
>
> **Pin 5 (T6 · micro-decision) — Sleeve style:** the evening sleeve — long, composed; nothing that flutters at the gate. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 6 (T7 · micro-decision) — Sleeve fabric:** to match or to contrast — the sleeve's quiet argument. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 7 (T8 · micro-decision) — Sleeve color:** the direction's echo at the wrist — gold, silver, or pale blue. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 8 (T9 · micro-decision) — Neckline:** the honest neckline — modest, unhurried, giving nothing to the gatekeepers. *5 coins.*
>
> *(Story morsel: Letitia through the bedroom door, on Almack's: "Bread and butter and orgeat, darling, and the thinnest cake in Christendom. Eat before you go. The Directresses feed no one and judge everyone.")*
>
> **Pin 9 (T10 · micro-decision) — Skirt style:** the assembly sweep — walking length, unhurried. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 10 (T11 · micro-decision) — Skirt fabric:** the evening's drape — silk that moves like certainty. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 11 (T12 · micro-decision) — Skirt color:** the ground, continued — the Directresses will read it at twenty paces. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 12 (T13 · micro-decision) — Trim:** the direction's motif — the Hartwell oak, plain severity, or figured sprigs — worked in the chosen thread. *5 coins.*
>
> *(Story morsel: the morning Gazette, read aloud over the pinning — Bell on the Season's opening: arch, exact, and not unkind. "Bell is watching, miss. Bell is always watching.")*
>
> **Pin 13 (T14 · micro-decision) — Shoes:** evening slippers — quiet on the assembly floor. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 14 (T15 · micro-decision) — Gloves:** immaculate — the evening's gauntlets. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 15 (T16 · micro-decision) — Hair:** the evening's architecture — dressed high, composed. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 16 (T17 · micro-decision) — Hair piece:** the direction's punctuation — Hartwell blue ribbon, silver comb, or pale blue. *5 coins.*
>
> *(Story morsel: a card slipped under the door, Augusta's hand — "Command, child. Not armor." She pins it inside the wardrobe door, where she will see it last.)*
>
> **Pin 17 (T18 · micro-decision) — Earrings:** evening-appropriate — pearl drops, gold hoops, or the ears' honest plainness. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 18 (T19 · micro-decision) — Pendant:** the throat's single sentence — gold, pearl, or nothing. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 19 (T20 · micro-decision) — Beauty:** the evening face — composed, rested, giving nothing to the gate. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 20 (T21 · micro-decision) — Finishing accessory:** the final touch — the reticule, the handkerchief, the last small decision. *5 coins.*
>
> *(Story morsel: the ribbon drawer, opened and considered — Hartwell blue, restraint. She closes it. Not yet. The ritual's last pin is not the evening's last choice.)*
>
> *(Reveal coda: the mirror — the assembly gown complete, the direction worn head to heel. Command, cut into silk. The signature look is saved to the lookbook.)*
>
> *(System: 20 pins × 5 coins = 100 coins. The completed direction is remembered. Owned jewels may be added freely from the open shelf, never as a step. No Crown SKU touches the ritual.)*
>
> **Jewels (owned pieces only):** the ritual's jewel step draws on pieces the player already owns — the jewel shelf itself is open at any time, never a ritual step. Evening-appropriate pieces — a simple pendant, a hair comb; the Gazette judges occasion-inappropriate jewels. No Crown SKU touches the ritual.

### L1.S19 · plot beat · Rose (alone)
*Purpose: ★ KEY DECISION 2/3 — THE SEALED NOTE (the hall-tray note's disposition). It stays sealed; what Rose does with it is the decision. (Turns: 3 — the decision)*
> *(After the assembly: the house asleep, the green silk hung, the Season's first verdict filed. And in the library's drawer, the note on heavy paper with the plain wax seal — unopened, unread, the clerk's careful hand still keeping its secret. She takes it out and holds it in the lamplight, and the wax catches the light the way a held breath catches in a quiet room.)*
> *(She has thought about it between the dances. Plain wax, no crest. The Network doesn't send notes — Lavinia said they send weather, and Letitia said the Network's stationery cupboard, and Augusta said nothing at all, which is Augusta's way of saying the most. Open it now and know. Burn it unread and be free of it. Lock it away and let the war decide the hour. Three dispositions, and the wax entire in all of them — because the seal is not hers to break on curiosity's schedule. The note will be opened when the war requires it. The only question is where it waits until then.)*
> *(She prices the waitings: the desk — locked in the campaign's drawer, the headquarters' own keeping, where paper remembers; the fire — unread, unkept, the discipline of refusal, the cleanest answer to an anonymous hand; the reticule — carried, the weight of it against her hip through the Season's weeks, the reminder that someone is writing. Each waiting is a different discipline. Each keeps the wax unbroken.)*
*★ KEY DECISION 2/3 — The sealed note:*
- **Lock it in the desk** — *The headquarters' keeping.* The campaign's drawer; paper remembers, and so will she.
- **Burn it unread** — *The discipline of refusal.* The cleanest answer to an anonymous hand; no hook, no handle.
- **Carry it in the reticule** — *The weight, kept close.* Through the Season's weeks, against her hip; the reminder that someone is writing.
> *(The game remembers the choice — and the means: the keeping, the refusal, or the carrying. The wax stays unbroken. The note waits on the war's schedule, not curiosity's.)*
*Animation: Shared.*

Be careful with great French names. She will be. Carefully is how she does everything.)*

### L1.S25 · plot beat · Rose (alone)
*Purpose: ★ KEY DECISION 3/3 — THE FIRST VERDICT (answering the Season's opening judgment). The nod is given; how Rose carries it is the decision. (Turns: 3 — the decision)*
> *(A week of the Season gone: the nod filed, the name spoken, the map drawn, the note sealed in its drawer. The town has read the papers and the town reads everything, and the town has made up its opening mind — the Directress's cool nod, Bell's arch approval, the empty chair at the opera. The Season's first verdict is in. It is favorable. It is also, Rose understands with the clarity of the second week, a leash — favorable verdicts have to be fed, and the town will watch forever to see if she was worth the nod.)*
> *(She stands at the library window with the week's reckoning in her hands and prices the carryings: wear it coolly — the verdict as armor, the town's good opinion held at arm's length, never fed, never courted; wear it warmly — the gladness accepted, the town's affection courted in return, the leash worn like a ribbon; wear it past — the verdict as yesterday's news, the campaign's true work the only answer, the town left to read what it likes. Each carrying is a different Season. Each is hers to choose.)*
> *(The destination is fixed — the confrontation coming, the account to be stated, the principal to be faced — but the weeks before it are hers to walk in her own manner. Augusta would say conduct. Letitia would say savor. Octavia would ask what it's for. She stands at the window with the Season's first verdict in her hands like a hand of cards, and chooses how to play it.)*
*★ KEY DECISION 3/3 — The first verdict:*
- **Wear it coolly** — *The verdict as armor.* The town's good opinion, held at arm's length; never fed, never courted.
- **Wear it warmly** — *The gladness, accepted.* The town's affection, courted in return; the leash worn like a ribbon.
- **Wear it past** — *Yesterday's news.* The campaign's true work the only answer; the town left to read what it likes.
> *(The game remembers the choice — and the means: the armor, the ribbon, or the past. The verdict is carried her way through the Season's weeks.)*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L1.S39 · Gazette sting
*Purpose: Bell's verdict on the fortnight — the watching, the winning; arch, precise; reward-only, never mystery. (Turns: 1 — the reading)*
> *(The morning Gazette, read over chocolate. Mrs. Nance Bell, proprietor, in her own dry voice:)*
> *"Miss Hartwell watches the town, and the town — belatedly, and with its better manners — watches her back. The Season's student has become its schoolmistress."*
> (T1 · stance) *Rose reads it twice — the amusement first (schoolmistress! the phrasing!), then the reckoning: the watching is public now, the schoolmistress named, and Bell has made it formidable, which is Bell's way of making it permanent. She files the sting under its lesson: in London, to be watched watching is to be taken seriously — and to be taken seriously is the whole of the game.* — remembered: *the lesson: the watched watcher is taken seriously.*
*Animation: Shared (sting card).*

### L1.S40 · cliffhanger · Rose, Letitia
*Purpose: the withheld nod — the Season's verdict, reconsidered; the chill, the cliffhanger. (Turns: 8)*
> *(The week's final ball: the rooms warm, the candles lit, the Season in full sail — and Rose, in the dove-grey of the watching weeks, crosses the floor toward the Directresses' corner with the fortnight's confidence in her step. The Directress who nodded — the cool nod, the Season's first verdict — looks up. The eyes meet hers. And pass over.)*
> (T1 · look closer) *the rooms — the warm, the candles; the crossing — the floor's, the confidence; the eyes — the meeting, the passing-over.* — remembered: *The passing-over, witnessed.*
> (T2 · remembered micro-decision) *feel the chill — the verdict's, the reconsidered; hold the step — the confidence's, the kept; note the corner — the Directresses', the stillness.* — remembered: *The chill, felt.*
> (T3 · stance) *as the lady — the passing-over, endured; as the campaigner — the reconsidering, priced; as the Hartwell — the floor, the held.* — remembered: *The floor, held.*
> "Darling —" Letitia, at her elbow, very quiet. "The nod. It didn't come." — Letitia
> (T4 · tone) *light — "Perhaps she blinked, Aunt."; steady — the didn't-come, the faced; dry — "The town reads everything, Aunt — including, it seems, other people's weather."* — remembered: *The steadiness, held.*
> (T5 · look closer) *Letitia's face — the quiet, the aunt's real arithmetic; the corner — the stillness, the maintained; the floor — the held, the danced-on.* — remembered: *The arithmetic, seen.*
> (T6 · social maneuver) *dance — the floor, the claimed; withdraw — the corner, the conceded; stand — the passing-over, the answered.* — remembered: *The dance, chosen.*
> (T7 · remembered micro-decision) *keep the chill — the reconsidered, filed; keep the confidence — the fortnight's, the kept; keep the question — the why, the burning.* — remembered: *The why, kept.*
> (T8 · dialogue) *(to Letitia) "Perhaps she blinked."; (to herself) "She didn't blink."; (to the chapter) the seal.* — remembered: *The seal, set.*
> *(She dances. The floor is claimed, the passing-over answered with the dove-grey's steady turning among the dancers, and the Directress's corner holds its stillness — but the nod, the cool nod, the Season's first verdict, did not come, and the why burns in her pocket like a second sealed note. The town reads everything. Including, it seems, other people's weather. The private danger Lavinia warned of has found its public face — or the beginning of one — and the chapter seals itself around the withheld nod the way wax seals a letter: unopened, unreadable, and entirely present.)*
> *The candles burn on, the ball turns, and Rose dances in dove-grey with the chill filed beside the confidence — and somewhere across the room, a Directress's eyes pass over her once more, deliberately, the way weather passes over a garden, and the Season's third week begins with a question: whose weather is this, and what does it want next.*
*Animation: Shared.*

## Chapter 2 — "The Library"

**STATUS: FULL DRAFT** — awaiting Beth review.

**Sources:** `ten-season-storyboard-beats.md` (Season 4 section — highest authority), `ten-season-plot-arc.md` (Season 4 section), `prose-standards.md` (locked standard), `season-one-chapters-1-3-pilot.md` (pilot file format), `season-one-chapter-04.md` (chapter anatomy).

**Format key.** [D] dialogue-heavy · [F] fashion-selection (2–3 diegetic options, no coin cost, remembered) · [C] dressing-for-climax ritual (direction + occasion brief, decomposed micro-decisions, story morsels, reveal coda) · [T] texture micro-beat · plot beat (untagged) · Gazette sting · cliffhanger. ★ marks the chapter's exactly 3 key decisions. Animation: Shared throughout — L2 is not a tentpole; the ritual uses the shared ritual presentation.

**Turns.** Every ordinary scene carries 7–10 numbered player turns `(T# · type)` inline — look closer, stance, tone, choice, social maneuver, dialogue, or remembered micro-decision — each closed with a remembered note. Outcomes converge: the game remembers the means (tone, relationship movement, Gazette flavor), never as a plot branch. ★ key-decision scenes carry the decision's 3 options as their turns; the Gazette sting is a single reading turn. The [C] ritual's direction + twenty 5-coin pins are its 21 turns; the ritual runs long and sits outside the 350–450-word scene budget.

**Canon applied:** 1815. The library is the house's paper memory — the war room. Lucien de Valcourt remains offstage: never appears, never speaks; his name may be spoken from the papers. In S4 he is the acquirer, nothing more. The banker's letter arrives in this chapter and remains SEALED through L2.S39 — carried, discussed, weighed, never opened. Mrs. Nance Bell is the Gazette's publicly named proprietor from the first edition. Rose carries no flowers for the confrontation arc. Iron rule holds: time earns everything money can. Crowns (premium) / coins (soft). Energy is the only throttle. No Crown SKU touches the ritual. The solicitor is Mr. Quill. Lady Agatha Drummond is absent; her held £5,000 installment hangs over the season.

**Occasion:** The library weeks — paper, patience, the money's map; the Season's first great ball. **Preparation ritual:** the ball gown — certainty, not apology. **Purse shelf:** the week's paper costs — candles, sealing wax, honesty.

**What changes for Rose:** The de Valcourt papers yield the name in full — Lucien de Valcourt, spoken aloud; Rackham's papers give up the émigré notes' trade; the money's map grows veins; the banker's letter arrives and stays sealed; the romance lanes divide further.

### L2.S1 · [T] · Rose (alone)
*Purpose: the war room, morning — the third week opens; the sorting's second wind. (Turns: 8)*
> *(The library at seven: the lamps not yet needed, the parallelogram of light already exact on the blotter, the desk's tide of her chosen order waiting where she left it. The withheld nod sits in her pocket like a stone — filed, not forgotten — and the Season's third week opens with the paper, the patience, and the question. What does it want next.)*
> (T1 · look closer) *the seven — the lamps', the unneeded; the parallelogram — the exact, the waiting; the tide — the left, the resumed.* — remembered: *The resuming, done.*
> (T2 · remembered micro-decision) *touch the stone — the pocket's, the filed; touch the desk — the tide's, the claimed; touch the pencil — the ear's, the ready.* — remembered: *The readiness, taken.*
> (T3 · stance) *as the general — the third week, the commanded; as the scholar — the paper, the patient; as the woman — the stone, the carried.* — remembered: *The command, held.*
> (T4 · tone) *quiet — the morning, the reckoning; fierce — the question, the filed; dry — "What does it want next, indeed. The paper will tell me, or I shall ask it until it does."* — remembered: *The asking, renewed.*
> (T5 · look closer) *the blotter — the light's, the exact; the boxes — the yielding, the continuing; the shelves — the knowing, the general's.* — remembered: *The room, greeted.*
> (T6 · social maneuver) *with the morning — the library's, the used; with the stone — the carried, the priced; with the week — the third, the opened.* — remembered: *The week, opened.*
> (T7 · remembered micro-decision) *sleeves back — the work's, the dove-grey's; pencil out — the ear's, the hand's; stone kept — the pocket's, the filed.* — remembered: *The work, begun.*
> (T8 · choice) *the de Valcourt vein — the struck, the followed; the émigré notes — the pennies', the traded; the margin's question — the answered, the pursued.* — remembered: *The vein, chosen.*
> *(She chooses the vein. The sleeves go back, the pencil comes out, the stone stays in the pocket — filed, not forgotten — and the library's third week begins the way the second ended: with a girl in dove-grey interrogating the dead. The de Valcourt papers wait in the clerk's chaos like a seam, and she follows it with the pencil, and the parallelogram of light holds steady on the blotter, and the question — what does it want next — sits at the margin of everything, unanswered and, for now, entirely hers.)*
> *Seven o'clock becomes eight, the tide spreads, and the war room hums its paper hum — and Rose works with the particular absorption of a woman who has decided that the paper will tell her, or she will ask it until it does.*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L2.S2 · [D] · Rose, Augusta
*Purpose: the Dowager's morning — the withheld nod, priced; the paper, the counsel. (Turns: 8)*
> *(Augusta has heard about the ball — the Dowager hears everything, usually before it happens — and the morning room at eleven holds the reckoning: the spectacles, the escritoire, the granddaughter summoned (not summoned) to account for a nod that didn't come.)*
> "The Directress looked through you, I am told. Tell me exactly, child — the ton's weather changes by the quarter-hour, and I price my intelligence fresh." — Augusta
> (T1 · look closer) *the told — the hearing's, the everything; the quarter-hour — the weather's, the changing; the fresh — the intelligence's, the priced.* — remembered: *The accounting, begun.*
> (T2 · tone) *exact — the passing-over, reported; dry — "She didn't blink, Grandmama."; steady — the reckoning, the faced.* — remembered: *The exactness, given.*
> "She didn't blink." Augusta considers this the way other women consider lace. "Then someone has been talking, and talking expensively. A nod is withdrawn for only two reasons, Rose: new information, or new money." — Augusta
> (T3 · remembered micro-decision) *hear the two — the information, the money; price the expensively — the talking's, the someone's; file it — the counsel's, the priced.* — remembered: *The two, filed.*
> (T4 · stance) *as the pupil — the pricing, the lesson; as the campaigner — the someone, the sought; as the granddaughter — the lace, the considered.* — remembered: *The lesson, taken.*
> "Which is it, Grandmama?" — Rose
> (T5 · dialogue) *"Which is it?"; "New information, or new money?"; "Teach me to tell the difference."* — remembered: *The teaching, sought.*
> "That is the paper's work, not mine. The library will tell you —" The spectacles lower. "If you ask it the right question. You have been asking what the money wants. Ask instead who has been buying the town's weather." — Augusta
> (T6 · look closer) *the lowering — the spectacles', the counsel's; the instead — the question's, the right; the buying — the weather's, the town's.* — remembered: *The instead, taken.*
> (T7 · social maneuver) *as the strategist — the buying, the traced; as the granddaughter — the counsel, the honored; as the campaigner — the right question, the asked.* — remembered: *The question, taken.*
> (T8 · remembered micro-decision) *curtsy — the morning room's full stop; carry the instead — the right question, the pocketed; leave the lace — the considered, the Dowager's.* — remembered: *She leaves questioned.*
> *(She leaves questioned. The instead goes into the pocket with the stone — who has been buying the town's weather — and the morning room releases her with the spectacles still lowered, which means the counsel is still being priced. New information, or new money. The library will tell her, if she asks the right question. She climbs toward the paper with the right question in her head like a new pencil, sharp and ready: who has been buying the town's weather.)*
> *The stairs are short, the question is long, and the war room waits with its tide and its vein and its margin's old question — and now, beside it, the Dowager's new one, the right one, the one that will turn the paper's interrogation toward the town itself.*
*Animation: Shared.*

The solicitor bends over a ledger with the professional's reverence for paper that surprises him.)*

The library's dust turns in the parallelogram of light. The name she has carried for a fortnight — de Valcourt, de Valcourt — puts on its Christian name like a coat, and the coat fits the way weather fits a garden: entirely.)*
> (T3 · tone) *steady — the full, the spoken; quiet — the room's, the witness; dry — "The acquirer. How obliging of Sir Giles to label that too."* — remembered: *The steadiness, held.*
> "Lucien de Valcourt." — Rose (aloud, for the room)
> (T4 · dialogue) *"Lucien de Valcourt."; "The London face — named, by the instrument himself."; "Mr. Quill — what does an acquirer acquire?"* — remembered: *The name, spoken.*

> (T5 · look closer) *the whatever — the sale's, the made-to-be; the solicitor's face — the professional's, the grim; the name — on the air now, the full.* — remembered: *The grim, noted.*

> (T7 · social maneuver) *detain him — the counsel's, the sought; release him — the afternoon's, the sorting; charge him — the watching, the continued.* — remembered: *He is released.*

Whatever is for sale. And whatever can be made to be. The name sits on the air in full now, Christian name and all, and Rose carries it downstairs like a new coin — heavier than the last one, and spent more carefully.)*
> *Mr. Quill's step fades, and the sorting goes on, because the war room does not stop for a full name, however long it has been coming — and the margin's question gains a new line in her hand: what does the acquirer want next.*

Rackham was the instrument. The trade was the instrument's work. And the paper — patient, comprehensive — gives up the dead man's thoroughness one sheet at a time, the way the dead give up everything: completely, and without comment.)*
> *The second box yields, the pencil moves, and the library holds the trade's anatomy in the parallelogram of light — and Rose, the scholar in dove-grey, stands over it the way a surgeon stands over a field table: interested, unflinching, and entirely without mercy for the patient.*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L2.S5 · [T] · Rose, Julian
*Purpose: the name in full — Julian hears "Lucien de Valcourt"; the fellow victim's weather. (Turns: 8)*

> "Lucien de Valcourt." Julian repeats it the way Laurent did — carefully, the funeral way. "I have heard that name, Rose. In the years when —" He stops. "In the years when my family was being unmade. It was spoken the way men speak weather." — Julian

> (T5 · look closer) *his face — the fellow victim's, the knowing; the air — the money's, the ruin's; the hand — the not-personal, the climate.* — remembered: *The knowing, seen.*

The vow is renewed — I fear for you, not the plan — and the map is shared, and the library holds the fellow victim's weather the way it holds everything: patiently, and without comment. They unmake the weather the families live in. Then we change the weather. The declaration sits in the room like a fourth box, and Julian's hand is warm in hers, and the fear — his, honored, filed — sits beside the plan like a second general at the map table.)*
> *He leaves the vow and takes his hat, and the name — Lucien de Valcourt, in full — stays on the air of the war room, and the Season's third week deepens with the weather coming to London, to this room, eventually.*

### L2.S6 · [D] · Rose, Laurent
*Purpose: the full name, spoken to Laurent — the French world, the fear, the care. (Turns: 8)*
> *(The square's garden, again — the sparrows, the peace, the privacy. She gives him the full name the way she gave Julian the telling: plainly, and as a friend. Lucien de Valcourt. The London face. The acquirer. The name lands among the sparrows like a hawk's shadow.)*
> "Lucien." Laurent says only the Christian name, and the garden goes quieter than quiet. "Chérie — you must understand. In our world, that name is —" He searches for the English. "It is a bell. It rings, and the émigrés count their losses." — Laurent

> (T2 · remembered micro-decision) *hear the bell — the losses', the counted; hear the search — the English's, the wanted; give him the quiet — the garden's, the permitted.* — remembered: *The quiet, given.*
> (T3 · tone) *gentle — the bell, the honored; direct — "Ring it for me, Laurent."; steady — the friend's, the asked.* — remembered: *The ringing, requested.*
> "The de Valcourts were bankers — great ones, before the Revolution. The London branch — Lucien's — rebuilt here, in the City, on émigré money and English patience." He looks at her. "Your papers say he is the acquirer. Chérie, in our world, everyone already knows. He acquires. It is what the bell means." — Laurent
> (T4 · dialogue) *"He acquires — what?"; "The bell's meaning, in full."; "And what does he want next, in your world?"* — remembered: *The meaning, asked.*
> "What is for sale." Laurent's smile is the terror's, polished. "And what can be made to be. Your Mr. Quill said it, no? The bell rings for houses, chérie. Great houses. Houses like —" He stops. The sparrows, indifferent, go about their business. "Be careful with the bell. That is all I am saying, and it is everything." — Laurent
> (T5 · look closer) *the polished — the terror's, the smiled; the stop — the houses-like, the unsaid; the everything — the all, the said.* — remembered: *The everything, priced.*
> (T6 · stance) *as the friend — the bell's, the honored; as the campaigner — the houses', the defended; as the Englishwoman — the directness's, the next.* — remembered: *The defense, readied.*
> "Houses like Hartwell Park." — Rose (quietly)
> (T7 · dialogue) *"Houses like mine."; "Say it, Laurent."; "The bell rings for my house."* — remembered: *The saying, done.*
> "I did not say it. You said it." He takes her hand, the Frenchman's comfort, the friend's. "And now I am frightened in two languages, which is the most French thing about me." — Laurent
> (T8 · remembered micro-decision) *keep the bell — the ringing, the filed; keep the two — the languages', the frightened; keep him — the lane's, the fear's, the friend's.* — remembered: *The friend, kept.*
> *(They walk. The sparrows resettle, the garden keeps its peace, and the bell — Lucien, the acquirer, the houses — rings softly in the French world behind Laurent's polished terror. Houses like Hartwell Park. She said it. He didn't. The distinction matters to him, the way distinctions matter to the French: enormously, and entirely. She keeps the friend, the fear, the two languages — and the bell, filed in the war room's drawer, ringing.)*
> *The garden gate clicks, the bow is perfect, and the lane deepens the way lanes do: not by decision, but by bell — rung, heard, and carried home in two languages.*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L2.S7 · [T] · Rose, Henry
*Purpose: the acquirer — Henry's reading; no legal shape, the soldier's counsel. (Turns: 8)*
> *(The Row again — the tan, the talk, the dismounted principle. She lays the full name before him the way she laid the map before Augusta: plainly, and for counsel. Lucien de Valcourt. The London face. The acquirer of what is for sale and what can be made to be.)*
> "An acquirer." Henry walks the horse a few steps, thinking the soldier's think. "In Spain, the men who acquired were the ones who never fired a shot. They bought the ground the battle would be fought on, then rented it to both armies." — Henry
> (T1 · look closer) *the steps — the few, the thinking; the bought — the ground's, the battle's; the rented — the both armies', the acquired.* — remembered: *The thinking, heard.*
> (T2 · tone) *interested — "Both armies, Henry?"; dry — "The soldier's cynicism suits you."; grave — the acquiring, the priced.* — remembered: *The interest, shown.*
> "It isn't cynicism, it's logistics. If he acquires what can be made to be for sale —" Henry stops the horse. "Then the war's shape is this: he will try to make Hartwell Park be for sale. Not by force. By weather. Credit tightened. Obligations called. The ground, bought out from under the battle." — Henry
> (T3 · remembered micro-decision) *hear the weather — the made-to-be's, the warned; hear the shape — the war's, the named; take the logistics — the soldier's, the filed.* — remembered: *The logistics, filed.*
> (T4 · stance) *as the pupil — the Spain's, the lesson; as the campaigner — the Park's, the defended; as the woman — the weather's, the faced.* — remembered: *The defense, readied.*
> "And the law, Henry? Is there no legal shape to any of this?" — Rose
> (T5 · dialogue) *"The law — where is it?"; "No magistrate, no commission — still?"; "Tell me the law has a shape, even if the war doesn't."* — remembered: *The law, asked after.*
> "The law has shapes, Rose. This war doesn't fit them." His hand is steady on the rein. "That is what troubles me — not the fighting, the formlessness. A soldier wants to know what he's defending." He looks at her. "I have decided. You. I am defending you. The form will follow, or it won't." — Henry
> (T6 · look closer) *the steady — the hand's, the rein's; the decided — the you's, the defended; the form — the following, the won't.* — remembered: *The decision, witnessed.*
> (T7 · social maneuver) *as the woman — the defended, the moved; as the campaigner — the formlessness, the used; as the friend — the Spain, the thanked.* — remembered: *The thanks, given.*
> (T8 · remembered micro-decision) *keep the decision — the you, the filed; keep the trouble — the formlessness's, the honored; keep him — the lane's, the soldier's.* — remembered: *He is kept.*
> *(They walk on. The Row's tan keeps the talk's time, the horse's hooves the measure, and Henry — the duty, the love, the steady hand — walks beside her having decided, which is the most soldierly thing and the most loverly: you. The form will follow, or it won't. She keeps the decision where she keeps the vows — filed, honored, and entirely without armor against it.)*
> *He remounts at the Row's end, the principle reversed, and the trot carries him off — and the war's shape, as the soldier reads it, goes home with her: the ground, bought out from under the battle. The weather, made. The Park, to be defended — not by law, which has no shape for this, but by her.*
*Animation: Shared.*

### L2.S8 · [T] · Rose, Octavia
*Purpose: the bell, the weather, the ground — Octavia's question, the for, again. (Turns: 8)*
> *(The library — Octavia's first visit to the war room, the painter come to see the sitter's studio. She moves among the tide of her chosen order with the particular respect of a woman who knows what work looks like, and stops before the map.)*

- **Follow the agents** — *The moving things.* The clerk, the callers, the plain carriages; watched until the watching becomes the knowing.

### L2.S15 · [C] · Rose (alone)
*Purpose: the ball ritual — direction + occasion brief, ~20 micro-decisions, story morsels between pins, reveal coda. (Turns: 21)*
> **Occasion brief (published):** "The Season's first great ball, this week — a lady of the Directresses' own set. The dress code: certainty, not command; the watching, veiled. The gown must move through the Network's London set unsuspected. **Editorial criterion: certainty.**"
>
> **A. The Night's Own** — midnight-blue sarcenet; the watching, veiled. Motif: no motif — the veil is the statement — the unsuspected, worn. Thread: **silver** (the veiled, edged) / **midnight silk** (the veiled, matched) / **ivory whitework** (the veiled, lit). Colorway: midnight-blue + silver + ivory. Accessories: silver hair comb; pearl drop earrings; no pendant — the throat, plain and unbought; white kid gloves.
> **B. The Season's Darling** — deep rose silk; the town, courted. Motif: figured sprigs, delicate — the darling, declared. Thread: **gold** (the darling, gilded) / **pale pink silk** (the darling, warmed) / **ivory whitework** (the darling, lit). Colorway: deep rose + gold + ivory. Accessories: gold hair pins; pearl drop earrings; a simple gold pendant; white kid gloves.
> **C. The Scholar** — silver-grey crepe; the mind, shown. Motif: plain strength — the interesting, stated — the scholar at a ball. Thread: **silver** (the interesting, edged) / **grey silk** (the interesting, matched) / **dove silk** (the interesting, softened). Colorway: silver-grey + silver + dove. Accessories: grey kid gloves; a small silver hair comb; a simple pearl pendant.
>
> *(System: commit to a direction — remembered for the ball. Then the pins begin. Each pin costs 5 coins; no Crown SKUs; jewels from owned pieces only, never a ritual step.)*

> (T1 · choice — the direction) *She commits: A, B, or C — the ball's thesis, remembered.*

> **Pin 2 (T3 · micro-decision) — Bodice cut:** the evening's architecture — fitted, honest, the certainty's foundation. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 3 (T4 · micro-decision) — Bodice fabric:** sarcenet, silk, or fine crepe — the evening's honest weaves. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 4 (T5 · micro-decision) — Bodice color:** the direction's ground — midnight-blue, deep rose, or silver-grey — certainty's canvas. *5 coins.*
>
> *(Story morsel: Élise's girl, pinning: "The watching goes in ball dress, miss. Madame says the best disguise at a ball is being the girl nobody watches — *nobody, miss.* The veil has opinions.")*
>
> **Pin 5 (T6 · micro-decision) — Sleeve style:** the evening sleeve — long, composed; nothing that flutters under watching. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 6 (T7 · micro-decision) — Sleeve fabric:** to match or to contrast — the sleeve's quiet argument. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 7 (T8 · micro-decision) — Sleeve color:** the direction's echo at the wrist — silver, gold, or dove. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 8 (T9 · micro-decision) — Neckline:** the honest neckline — modest, unhurried, giving nothing to the watched. *5 coins.*
>
> *(Story morsel: Letitia through the bedroom door, on the ball: "A lady of the Directresses' own set, darling — the cake will be thin, the company thick, and the box — the taken box — will be watched by everyone. Including us. Especially us.")*

> **Pin 10 (T11 · micro-decision) — Skirt fabric:** the evening's drape — sarcenet that moves like certainty. *5 coins.*
> **Pin 11 (T12 · micro-decision) — Skirt color:** the ground, continued — the room will read it at twenty paces, and read nothing else. *5 coins.*

### L2.S19 · plot beat · Rose (alone)
*Purpose: ★ KEY DECISION 2/3 — THE SEALED LETTER (the banker's letter's disposition). Weighed and discussed, never opened — what Rose does with it is the decision. (Turns: 3 — the decision)*
> *(After the ball: the house asleep, the midnight-blue hung, the clerk filed, the picket's anatomy priced. And in the war room's drawer, the two sealed things — the plain wax, the banker's wafer, Bath and nowhere — waiting on the war's schedule. She takes out the banker's letter and holds it in the lamplight, and the wafer catches the light the way the plain wax did: like a held breath, in a quiet room.)*
> *(She has weighed it — the hand's heft, the money's trouble. She has discussed it — Augusta's counsel, the right question, who has been buying the town's weather. She has not opened it. The discipline holds: some paper is opened when the war requires it, not when curiosity does. But the letter cannot sit in the drawer forever being weighed — the campaigner's question is what to do with a sealed thing in a watching season. Three dispositions, and the wafer entire in all of them.)*
> *(She prices the waitings: carry it to Augusta's — the Dowager's counsel, the wafer weighed by the surest hands in London, the morning room's pricing; keep it in the war room — the headquarters' keeping, beside the plain wax, the paper's own memory holding the paper's own secrets; take it to Mr. Quill — the solicitor's eye, the professional's reading of the wafer, the weight, the Bath postmark, the unopened. Each waiting is a different counsel. Each keeps the wafer unbroken — through this chapter's end and beyond, the seal's law holding.)*
*★ KEY DECISION 2/3 — The sealed letter:*
- **Carry it to Augusta's** — *The Dowager's counsel.* The wafer weighed by the surest hands in London; the morning room's pricing.
- **Keep it in the war room** — *The headquarters' keeping.* Beside the plain wax; the paper's own memory holding the paper's own secrets.
- **Take it to Mr. Quill** — *The solicitor's eye.* The professional's reading of the wafer, the weight, the postmark — unopened.
> *(The game remembers the choice — and the means: the Dowager, the headquarters, or the solicitor. The wafer stays unbroken. The letter waits on the war's schedule, not curiosity's.)*

### L2.S39 · Gazette sting
*Purpose: Bell's verdict on the watching weeks — the watched watcher; arch, precise; reward-only, never mystery. The banker's letter stays sealed. (Turns: 1 — the reading)*
> *(The morning Gazette, read over chocolate. Mrs. Nance Bell, proprietor, in her own dry voice:)*
> *"Miss Hartwell has spent a fortnight watching, and the town — flattered to be looked at so hard — has taken to watching her. London has never been so well observed, nor so well dressed for it."*
> (T1 · stance) *Rose reads it twice — the amusement first (so well dressed for it! the phrasing!), then the reckoning: the watching is mutual now, the observed observed, and Bell has made it formidable, which is Bell's way of making it permanent. The banker's letter sits sealed in the war room's drawer through it all — weighed, discussed, unopened — and she files the sting under its lesson: in London, the watcher who is watched has arrived — and arrival is the whole of the game.* — remembered: *the lesson: the watched watcher has arrived.*
*Animation: Shared (sting card).*

## Chapter 3 — "Émigré Paper"

**STATUS: FULL DRAFT** — awaiting Beth review.

**Sources:** `ten-season-storyboard-beats.md` (Season 4 section — highest authority), `ten-season-plot-arc.md` (Season 4 section), `prose-standards.md` (locked standard), `season-one-chapters-1-3-pilot.md` (pilot file format), `season-one-chapter-04.md` (chapter anatomy).

**Format key.** [D] dialogue-heavy · [F] fashion-selection (2–3 diegetic options, no coin cost, remembered) · [C] dressing-for-climax ritual (direction + occasion brief, decomposed micro-decisions, story morsels, reveal coda) · [T] texture micro-beat · plot beat (untagged) · Gazette sting · cliffhanger. ★ marks the chapter's exactly 3 key decisions. Animation: Shared throughout — L3 is not a tentpole; the ritual uses the shared ritual presentation.

**Turns.** Every ordinary scene carries 7–10 numbered player turns `(T# · type)` inline — look closer, stance, tone, choice, social maneuver, dialogue, or remembered micro-decision — each closed with a remembered note. Outcomes converge: the game remembers the means (tone, relationship movement, Gazette flavor), never as a plot branch. ★ key-decision scenes carry the decision's 3 options as their turns; the Gazette sting is a single reading turn. The [C] ritual's direction + twenty 5-coin pins are its 21 turns; the ritual runs long and sits outside the 350–450-word scene budget.

**Canon applied:** 1815. The émigré notes, the Bath banker's ledgers, the de Valcourt papers — the money's map, completed. The Network's London interests are named: who profited from the Hartwells' ruin, and what the principal wants next — Hartwell Park itself, and Rackham's scattered paper suppressed. Lucien de Valcourt remains offstage: never appears, never speaks. In S4 he is the acquirer, nothing more. The banker's letter stays sealed (the war's schedule has not required it). Mr. Silas Vane is introduced as an agent — the brass plate is read at L3.S40: "Mr. Silas Vane — Agent — London." The solicitor is Mr. Quill — never confuse them. Mrs. Nance Bell is the Gazette's publicly named proprietor from the first edition. Rose carries no flowers for the confrontation arc. Iron rule holds: time earns everything money can. Crowns (premium) / coins (soft). Energy is the only throttle. No Crown SKU touches the ritual. Lady Agatha Drummond is absent; her held £5,000 second installment hangs over the season.

**Occasion:** The émigré paper's week — counting-houses, ledgers, the agent's trail. **Preparation ritual:** the counting-house gown — purpose, not ornament. **Purse shelf:** the week's paper costs — candles, sealing wax, honesty.

**What changes for Rose:** The Bath banker's ledgers arrive; the London interests are named aloud — who profited, what the principal wants next; the agent's trail warms from the clerk to the deciding-elsewhere; the brass plate is read.

### L3.S2 · plot beat · Rose, Mr. Quill
*Purpose: the Bath banker's ledgers — they arrive; the paper's week, begun. (Turns: 8)*
> *(The morning post, and with it Mr. Quill himself — the solicitor in person, which means the delivery matters. He carries a parcel the size of a family Bible, wrapped in oilcloth, tied with cord: the Bath banker's ledgers, sent up to London by the smaller concern, the enclosures' elder brother.)*
> "The ledgers, Miss Hartwell." Mr. Quill sets the parcel down the way men set down things that are heavy in both senses. "Sent by the Bath house. With their compliments — and their conditions." — Mr. Quill
> (T1 · look closer) *the in-person — the solicitor's, the mattering; the oilcloth — the wrapped, the corded; the setting — the down's, the both-senses.* — remembered: *The delivery, received.*

> "The conditions are these, miss: the ledgers are lent, not given. They are read here, in this room, in my presence or yours. And —" The professional's pause. "They are not copied. The house's hand, the house's eyes only." — Mr. Quill
> (T3 · remembered micro-decision) *hear the lent — the not-given's, the priced; hear the here — the room's, the presence's; hear the not-copied — the hand's, the eyes'.* — remembered: *The conditions, heard.*
> (T4 · stance) *as the client — the lent, the accepted; as the scholar — the read-here, the welcomed; as the campaigner — the not-copied, the memorized-around.* — remembered: *The accepting, done.*

> (T5 · dialogue) *"We read — and we remember."; "Lent is enough, solicitor."; "The house's eyes, then — open them."* — remembered: *The remembering, vowed.*
> (T6 · look closer) *the unwrapping — the oilcloth's, the cord's; the ledgers — the lent, the heavy; the vow — the remembering's, the made.* — remembered: *The vow, made.*

> *(She begins the week. The oilcloth is folded, the cord coiled, the first ledger opened under the solicitor's presence — and the Bath banker's hand lies open on the desk like a patient etherized: the smaller concern's whole memory, lent to London, read here, not copied. We read — and we remember. The vow sits in the war room like a third sealed thing, and the paper's week begins in earnest: the ledgers, the lent, the remembering.)*
> *Mr. Quill takes the chair opposite — the presence, honored — and the lamp is lit against the morning's insufficiency, and Rose bends over the Bath hand with the pencil behind her ear and the remembering already begun.*

"There. The London interest." — Mr. Quill
> (T6 · look closer) *the flatness — the professional's, the honest; the never-lifted — the panic's, the sellers'; the there — the finger's, the London interest's.* — remembered: *The there, noted.*
